2026-10-10T05:06:39.9858173Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Retry page-size-64 FA3 on P3/D1 at c66 to hold the decode batch near c72/page-1

Re-apply page size 64 with explicit FA3 (37ed220, reverted in dfdee33)
and lower client concurrency from 72 to 66; nothing else changes
(P3/D1, decode pool 393216, Triton decode MoE, EAGLE3, FP8 KV, image).

Why: in 37ed220 (Actions 38023851853, dgx007/dgx013) page 64 shortened
TTFT (mean 769 -> 554 ms, p90 1253 -> 863 ms) and removed the co-located
prefill lag (prefill-2 inflight 2.9 -> 1.5, equal to the other workers),
consistent with fewer, larger NIXL transfer descriptors. Faster prefill at
the same c72 left more requests decoding (running 66.5 vs 61.6), so ITL
rose to 19.04 ms and the run failed the SLA gate (73.81% good). The
one-GPU probe showed FA3 decode-verify attention 16-19% faster at page 64
for batch 64/96. Closed-loop residence suggests ~c66 brings the decode
batch back to ~61, where page 1 gave 17.18 ms ITL, so the page-64 decode
effect can be judged at a comparable batch while keeping the prefill
gain. The node pair cannot be pinned without long queues; record it and
the running batch with the result.

Budget: myprojects 59010014 checked immediately before this push (see
session log); this job reserves at most 1024 SU.

Validation: full CPU pytest suite with page size 64, FA3 and c66.
